# v18 missing and invalid assets

This is the **original-installation baseline before repairs**. The remaining findings after applying the composed local overlay are in `repaired_assets/MISSING_ASSETS_V18.md/.json`. Applied edits and their hashes are documented in `ASSET_REPAIRS_V18.md` and `ASSET_REPAIRS_VALIDATION_V18.json`.

No unavailable proprietary asset has been reconstructed without geometry, rig, animation or artwork source. Referenced local assets remain external. All runtime behavior is unverified.
